- **sales-hire-scorecard**: Use this skill before interviews start for a sales, customer success or other GTM hire, and again after each interview to score candidates against it. Writes the motion of the seat, builds a scorecard of six to eight motion-fit criteria, force-ranked and weighted by the team, each with an owned interview question and anchored 1 to 4 scoring, then scores candidates on evidence from what they actually did, not where they did it. Ends with a ranked summary and one Yes/No decision for a human. Handles one candidate or a whole shortlist.
